13 sản phẩm Pet Bedding: variant và giá bán

Liệt kê tất cả variant của 13 sản phẩm, kèm title + mô tả HTML để dán vào website và prompt tạo ảnh có nút copy. Toàn bộ thông tin lấy từ CJ, không tự thêm. Dòng tô xanh ★ là SKU gốc bạn gửi.

13sản phẩm
467variant
$11.99–$129.99khoảng giá bán
35–46%lãi sau ~5% phí
219prompt tạo ảnh
Cách tính
  • Ship US: tuyến rẻ nhất giao tối đa 15 ngày (LuWei Ordinary US hoặc YunExpress Ordinary; không dùng tuyến hàng pin/chất lỏng/nhạy cảm), 1 cái. Không dùng CJPacket Eub (12–50 ngày).
  • Landed = giá vốn CJ + ship. Khách được miễn phí ship.
  • Giá bán = landed ÷ 0.60, làm tròn lên .99. Riêng #12 và #13: landed ÷ 0.50 (đã đồng ý nâng giá lên $49–59).
  • Prompt ảnh: STL_01 tạo 1 ảnh cho mỗi mẫu (màu/kiểu khác nhau). STL_02 trong nút "Copy toàn bộ prompt" chỉ lấy 2 mẫu (mẫu chính + mẫu nhiều variant nhất); mẫu khác vẫn có prompt STL_02 riêng để copy nếu muốn làm thêm. Cùng mẫu khác size dùng chung ảnh. STL_03, STL_04, STL_05 chỉ 1 bộ cho mỗi sản phẩm, dựa trên STL_01 của mẫu chính; STL_05 gồm tất cả cỡ. Variant set/combo chỉ báo giá, không có prompt.
  • Lãi = (giá bán × 95% − landed) ÷ giá bán. 5% là phí cổng thanh toán ước tính.
#1 Nệm tròn lông dài cho mèo/chó#2 Ổ nệm lông tròn (Plush pet nest)#3 Chăn/thảm lông san hô in hình#4 Giường chó (Dog Bed)#5 Ổ thú cưng hình ếch xanh#6 Ổ nệm 2 trong 1 lông dài#7 Ổ nửa kín ghép vải, xách tay được#8 Giường sofa lông xù cho chó#9 Ổ mèo hình cây thông Noel#10 Nệm lụa lạnh mùa hè#11 Thảm silicone chống nước#12 Thảm tự giữ ấm (self-warming)#13 Nệm sofa bảo vệ nội thất (có gối)
#1 · CJJJCWMY00276

Nệm tròn lông dài cho mèo/chó

Round Long Hairy Autumn And Winter Nest Pad Cat Mattress

Pet Supplies > Pet Bedding > Pet Nests · 97 variant · 15 mẫu · Xem trên CJ ↗

Thông tin từ CJ

  • Chất liệu: vải plush siêu mềm, ruột bông PP; lông dày 4 cm
  • Đáy có chấm keo chống trượt
  • Giặt máy hoặc giặt tay
  • CJ: phù hợp từ mèo tới chó lớn
  • Đường kính ngoài 40–110 cm

Ghi chú

  • Cùng cỡ 60cm nhưng giá vốn khác nhau: A green $4.11, các màu khác $6.59.

Xem trước mô tả

Round Long Plush Pet Bed – Anti-Slip, Machine Washable Cat & Dog Nest Pad

A round nest pad made of super soft, very warm plush with high-quality PP cotton for deep, comfortable sleep.

Key Features

  • 4 cm plush: Super soft and very warm.
  • Anti-slip bottom: Glue-dot design, anti-slip and durable.
  • Easy care: Machine or hand washable.
  • Pet friendly: Non-toxic, odorless materials.

Specifications

Product typeCat bed / kennel pad
MaterialCloth (plush), PP cotton
SizesOuter diameter 40–120 cm
Suitable forCats to large dogs

Package Includes

1 × bed

Note: Dry in the sun from time to time to keep the plush soft and fluffy.

Pet Supplies > Pet Bedding > Pet Nests · 98 variant · 14 mẫu · Xem trên CJ ↗

Thông tin từ CJ

  • Chất liệu: vải (Cloth)
  • Đường kính ngoài 40 / 50 / 60 / 70 / 80 / 100 cm
  • 13 màu theo mô tả CJ

Ghi chú

  • Giữ lại để test (theo yêu cầu).
  • Cỡ 100cm và 120cm rất nặng (3.55 kg / 4.55 kg) nên phí ship US cao ($47–60), giá bán $106–130. Nên cân nhắc chỉ bán tới 80cm.
  • CJ ghi cân nặng 70cm (1.00 kg) nhẹ hơn 60cm (1.55 kg), nên ship 70cm rẻ hơn 60cm. Nên hỏi lại CJ.

Cute Printed Pet Blanket Mat – Soft Coral Fleece, Easy to Wash, Anti-Pilling

A soft coral fleece blanket mat with reactive printing and a fine texture.

Key Features

  • Fine print: Reactive printing and dyeing.
  • Easy care: Comfortable, easy to wash, not easy to pill.
  • Many styles: Several prints to choose from.

Specifications

Product typePet blanket mat
MaterialCoral cashmere (coral fleece)

Size Guide

SizeDimensions
S50 × 30 cm
M55 × 45 cm
L70 × 55 cm
XL100 × 75 cm

Blue starry sky comes in 30 × 40, 50 × 40, 50 × 50, 50 × 80 and 80 × 100 cm.

Perfect For

  • Sofa or bed cover
  • Crate mat
  • Travel blanket

Package Includes

1 × pet blanket mat

Cute Frog Pet Bed – Warm Plush Nest for Small Cats & Dogs up to 5 kg, Non-Slip

A cute cartoon frog shape bed that keeps small pets warm on cold days.

Key Features

  • Frog design: Round nest, half shed nest and green nest styles.
  • Soft filling: Highly elastic PP cotton and plush bubble velvet.
  • Non-slip bottom: Rubber particles stop the bed sliding when pets jump in.

Specifications

Product typePet bed
MaterialPlush bubble velvet, PP cotton
SizesS, M, L, XL
Suitable forSmall cats and dogs within 5 kg

Perfect For

  • Cat bed
  • Small dog bed

Package Includes

1 × pet bed

2 in 1 Round Plush Pet Bed – Warm Winter Bed for Cats & Dogs, Soft PP Cotton

A round plush bed that brings comfort and warmth to cats and dogs in winter.

Key Features

  • Warm for winter: Warm PP cotton and plush.
  • Soft inner layer: Inner plush or crystal velvet options.
  • For cats and dogs: Suitable for both cats and dogs.

Specifications

Product typePet bed
MaterialWarm PP cotton / plush; inner plush or crystal velvet
Sizes40 cm, 50 cm, 65 cm (height 2 cm); some colors also 60, 80, 100 cm
Suitable forCats and dogs

Perfect For

  • Winter bed for cats
  • Warm bed for small and medium dogs

Package Includes

1 × pet bed

Xem trước mô tả

Portable Semi-Enclosed Pet Nest – Warm Striped Plush Cat & Small Dog Bed

A semi-enclosed pet nest made of soft materials that gives your pet a safe, private place to rest.

Key Features

  • Semi-enclosed: Safe rest space with privacy.
  • Easy to clean: Just gently shake or wipe.
  • Decorative: Fits living room, bedroom or balcony.

Specifications

Product typePet nest
MaterialStriped plush

Size Guide

SizeDimensionsPet weight
M40 × 30 × 28 cmunder 5 kg
L50 × 40 × 34 cmunder 10 kg

Perfect For

  • Cat bed
  • Small dog bed

Package Includes

1 × pet nest

Fluffy Round Pet Bed – Winter Warm Dog & Cat Bed, Diameter 50–110 cm

A fluffy, winter-warm round bed in five sizes, chosen by your pet's weight.

Key Features

  • Fluffy and warm: Made for winter.
  • Five sizes: Diameter 50 to 110 cm.

Specifications

Christmas Tree Pet House – Soft Cat & Small Dog Bed with Resilient Sponge

A Christmas tree shaped pet house for cats and dogs.

Key Features

  • Holds its shape: Compressive, high-resilience sponge.
  • Soft: High-quality PP cotton and soft short plush.
  • Festive designs: Red Christmas hat, green Christmas tree or star type.

Specifications

Product typePet house
MaterialSponge, PP cotton, short plush fabric
Suitable forCats and dogs

Size Guide

Dimensions
35 × 35 × 45 cm
43 × 43 × 50 cm
50 × 50 × 55 cm

Perfect For

  • Christmas gift
  • Cat house

Package Includes

1 × pet house

Xem trước mô tả

Ice Silk Pet Cooling Pad – Cold Summer Mat for Dogs & Cats with Breathable Mesh Back, Multiple Sizes

A three-layer cooling pad with an ice silk surface that gives your pet an instant cool feeling on hot days, without water or refrigeration.

Key Features

  • Instant cooling: Heat-resistant fibers give a cool feeling without relying on water.
  • 3-layer build: Cold ice silk surface, cotton middle and breathable mesh back.
  • Neat finish: Fine stitching and routing details.
  • For pets of all sizes: Sizes from 40 × 30 cm to 150 × 100 cm.

Specifications

Product typePet cooling pad
LayersIce silk surface · cotton middle · breathable mesh back
ColorsBlue, brown, pink, gray, light blue

Size Guide

SizeDimensionsWeight
S50 × 40 cm100 g
M63 × 50 cm150 g
L70 × 56 cm200 g
XL102 × 70 cm320 g

Also available in 40 × 30 cm and 150 × 100 cm.

Perfect For

  • Summer sleeping pad
  • Crate or bed topper
  • Cool spot for cats and dogs

Package Includes

1 × cushion

Note: Measured by hand: size may vary by 2–3 cm and weight by 10–20 g.

Xem trước mô tả

Waterproof Silicone Pet Mat – Easy-Clean Dog & Cat Feeding Placemat

A waterproof silicone mat that is easy to clean.

Key Features

  • Waterproof: Silicone keeps spills on the mat.
  • Easy to clean: Wipe clean.

Specifications

Product typePet mat / placemat
MaterialSilicone
Suitable forDogs and cats

Size Guide

SizeDimensions
Small48 × 30 cm
Large60.5 × 40.5 cm

Perfect For

  • Under food and water bowls

Package Includes

1 × mat

Xem trước mô tả

Self-Warming Pet Mat – Thick Corduroy Fleece Cat & Dog Bed, No Power Needed

A self-warming mat that absorbs, holds and reflects your pet's body heat. No external power needed.

Key Features

  • Self-warming: Reflects body heat to reduce heat loss.
  • Soft fabric: Classic corduroy fleece.
  • Quiet and non-slip: Noiseless mylar film and non-slip bottom.
  • Versatile: Crates, sofas, beds and travel.

Specifications

Product typePet mat
MaterialCorduroy fleece, mylar film

Size Guide

Size
61 × 45 cm
76 × 51 cm
89 × 58 cm
104 × 73 cm

Perfect For

  • Crate mat
  • Sofa or bed
  • Travel

Package Includes

1 × pet mat

Xem trước mô tả

Plush Dog Sofa Bed with Pillow – Washable Pet Couch Cover & Furniture Protector for Large Dogs

A soft plush pad with a built-in pillow that gives your dog a warm place to curl up and protects your sofa from hair and urine.

Key Features

  • Curved pillow design: Gives pets a warm, safe space to curl up.
  • Protects furniture: Helps protect the sofa from hair, urine and damage.
  • Warm and soft: Soft plush keeps your dog off the cold floor.
  • Washable: Hand or machine washable.

Specifications

Product typePet sofa pad with pillow
MaterialCloth (plush)
Sizes75 × 75, 75 × 120, 90 × 90, 90 × 120, 90 × 135 cm
ColorsLight gray, dark gray, coffee, blue, beige, gray-black, red-black, gray-coffee
Suitable forDogs

Perfect For

  • Sofa and couch cover
  • Dog bed on the floor
  • Protecting furniture from pet hair

Package Includes

1 × pet pad
